[340:42.12] let's start with understanding why
[340:43.96] should you use terraform there are some
[340:46.52] great pre-existing tools like anible
[340:49.12] Chef puppet and sstack but then what
[340:52.28] makes terraform better than these tools
[340:55.40] first reason is that terraform is a
[340:57.64] server orchestration tool all the above
[341:00.36] mentioned tools were created for Server
[341:02.76] configuration meaning that their primary
[341:05.04] goal is to install and manage software
[341:07.36] Fair on the already existing server
[341:09.84] terraform concentrates more on server
[341:12.24] provisioning with software container
[341:14.40] deployment left to Docker and Packer the
[341:17.80] next reason is the immutable
[341:20.24] infrastructure which chefs salt puppet
[341:22.84] or anible any software update must be
[341:25.68] run in place thus every server grows a
[341:29.04] unique record of updates throughout its
[341:31.00] life cycle this can quite often lead to
[341:33.48] so-called configuration drift when the
[341:35.64] differences in these configuration s
[341:37.68] Lead to bugs terraform addresses the
[341:40.56] issues by utilizing an immutable
[341:43.12] infrastructure approach where every new
[341:45.68] update of any parameter creates a
[341:48.00] separate configuration snapshot this way
[341:51.00] updating the development environment
[341:52.96] goes smoothly and is completely bug
[341:55.32] proof the next reason is the declarative
[341:58.28] code style while Chef or anible forces
[342:01.60] you to write step-by-step procedural
[342:03.64] instructions for reaching the desired
[342:05.80] State terraform defer describing the
[342:08.48] desired each state of the system and the
[342:11.24] tool itself deals with reaching the goal
[342:14.04] state but why is it better with the
[342:16.84] procedural code you need to think of all
[342:19.28] the recent events and processes that
[342:21.60] took place in order to write clear
[342:23.92] instructions but with terraform you

[342:30.36] system this is why the code base remains
